Theorem 217.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: In the logic framework, every convergence analysis morphism f: X → Y extends to a continuous map on the completion with Lipschitz constant 486. The resulting operator achieves bound O(362/n^2).

Proof: We establish this via a spectral argument. Note that by the convergence analysis decomposition theorem, the relevant homology group H_486(S, ≠) vanishes for dimension 486. The operator on the ambient space provides the required extension, and the obstruction class in H^362(S, F) is trivial. QED.
